# Word-quiz option audit
command: python word_quiz_audit.py <repo at the candidate> 丹念 乙種 亜熱帯 仁 仙人 歌舞伎 降伏 伐採 伯仲 同伴
pool: 5194 word articles (the app's All word stack); reading-quiz pool: 4110 words with a reading to learn
JMdict: pinned JMdict_e.gz (lean cache)
Python port of WordQuizBuilder.select (meaning quizzes) and ReadingOptions.build (reading quiz) in evidence\app-code\Words.kt.
A word in a tier that ties at the last needed slot is drawn at random; every listed tier member can appear.
